# EDU 360 V4.0 — Deliverable 5 · API & Integration Specification

**Edition:** V4.0 Final — Independently Validated and Corrected · 30 October 2026
**Baseline carried forward:** Corrected Document 2 (API Contract), edition C1, baseline V3.3 (27 Oct 2026), reproduced in full below with V4.0 corrections marked *(V4.0)*.
**Machine-readable contract:** `EDU360_V4.0_05_OpenAPI.yaml` — OpenAPI 3.1.0, `info.version` 4.0.0, 313 paths, 408 operations, 349 component schemas (V3.3 contract: 310 / 404 / 345; V4.0 adds the 4 role roll-down operations and 4 schemas); validated with openapi-spec-validator 0.9.0 on 30 Oct 2026.
**Status:** Released for engineering handover, subject to the open decisions in §11 (in particular OD-74, OD-75, OD-76, OD-78, OD-80 / OD-10 and the OD-119 mapping review). A documented endpoint is not an implemented endpoint: no API request has been executed because no application exists.

## V4.0 revision summary

| ID | Change | Why (finding) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| V4-API-01 | `x-error-codes` filled for every operation. 367 of 404 operations had an empty list, so "every operation's codes checked against the catalogue" was true only trivially. Codes are derived from the database rules on each operation's `x-tables` (trigger → raised code → §6.5 mapping), from the method (PATCH → `IF_MATCH_REQUIRED`, `VERSION_CONFLICT`; create → `DUPLICATE_RESOURCE`), idempotency class and decision operations (`ALREADY_ACTIONED`, `NOT_CURRENT_APPROVER`). The 54 single-resource reads that remain empty return only the shared 401/403/404/429/5xx responses. | V4-F-22 | 0 codes outside the `ErrorCode` enum; validator pass |
| V4-API-02 | Two database errors added to the §6.5 mapping (`APPROVER_IS_SUBJECT`, `APPROVER_ROLE_MISSING`), raised by the V4.0 rule V4-DB-01 behind `setPrincipalLeaveApprover`, `setVcLeaveApprover`, `setDpcLeaveApprover`. No new API code was needed; both map to `BUSINESS_RULE_VIOLATION` with a `context.reason`. | V4-F-17 | DB-77, DB-78 |
| V4-API-03 | `GATEWAY_EVENT_IMMUTABLE` row annotated: the database raises the case as `APPEND_ONLY`. | V4-F-23 | `02_rules.sql`, `fn_gateway_event_guard` |
| V4-API-04 | Schema count stated once as 345 (the 344 in historical finding F-03 is annotated). | V4-F-05 (was GAP-006) | YAML parse |
| V4-API-05 | Appendix A regenerated from the V4.0 YAML, with the error-code column added; 404 rows, identical method / path / operationId set to the YAML (0 differences both ways). | V4-F-24 | §13 |
| V4-API-07 | **Role roll-down** (decision V4-PD-01): `getRolldownMap`, `listRolldownOverrides` (ROLLDOWN_VIEW), `setRolldownOverride`, `revokeRolldownOverride` (ROLLDOWN_OVERRIDE_SET); schemas `RolldownMapEntry`, `RolldownOverride`, `RolldownOverrideCreate`, `RolldownRevoke`. Database errors `ROLLDOWN_*` map to existing codes (§6.5); the 89-code catalogue is unchanged. Any action an approver takes through roll-down returns and records `actingForRole`. | V4-PD-01, V4-F-31 | Validator; DB-80–DB-97 |
| V4-API-06 | `info.version` 3.3.0-baseline → 4.0.0; V3.3 description kept as `x-v33-description`. Existing paths, operations, parameters and schemas are unchanged (V4.0 only adds), so V3.3 clients remain compatible. | Versioning | YAML |

# EDU 360 — API Contract Specification

Corrected edition C1 · Baseline V3.3 · 27 October 2026

## 0. About this edition

This is the corrected Document 2 for EDU 360: the internal API contract for Phase 1, rebuilt against **Version 3.3**. It replaces the supplied file, whose first 5,871 lines were a copy of Document 1 and which held only Part 5 of the API text; Parts 1–4 were not in it (F-01, F-02). The contract below is generated from one operation inventory, and the OpenAPI file produced from it passes `openapi-spec-validator` 0.9.0.

<div class="kpis">
<div class="kpi"><div class="v">404</div><div class="l">operations on 310 paths (OpenAPI 3.1.0, validated)</div></div>
<div class="kpi"><div class="v">234 / 234</div><div class="l">Phase 1 V3.3 actions with at least one operation (219 / 219 for V3.1)</div></div>
<div class="kpi"><div class="v">2,758</div><div class="l">API fields mapped to a Document 1 column; 0 unmapped, 0 enum mismatches</div></div>
<div class="kpi"><div class="v">89</div><div class="l">error codes in one catalogue; every operation's codes checked against it</div></div>
</div>

**Status: corrected draft for Product Owner review, not approved.** Marks: <span class="b warn">proposed</span> a technical decision no source makes (TD-xx, PTD-xx); <span class="b bad">open</span> a decision not yet made (OD-xx). "Validated" means the OpenAPI file passed the validator and the coverage scripts. **No API was run**: there is no implementation, and no request was ever sent to one.

### 0.1 What changed from the supplied Document 2

| Area | Supplied | This edition |
|---|---|---|
| Content | Part 5 only, after a full copy of Document 1 | Complete, self-contained contract; Document 1 referenced, not repeated |
| Endpoints | Parts 2–4 tables not supplied; OpenAPI excerpt of 14 operations that does not parse (N-05) | 404 operations covering all 234 Phase 1 actions |
| Baseline | V3.1 (219 actions) | V3.3 (234 actions); V3.1 coverage shown |
| Errors | Catalogue in §5.62.2; per-operation codes not checkable | Same catalogue as an `ErrorCode` enum, plus GO_LIVE_BLOCKED; generator refuses unknown codes |
| Idempotency | 21 scopes (§5.64) | Same 21 names kept; 3 REQUIRED and 31 create scopes added; same list loaded into `ref.idempotency_scope` |
| Document 1 changes it depended on | 12 changes "proposed, not applied" (§5.67.4) | All applied in corrected Document 1 (X-9) |

### 0.2 Companion files

`EDU360_V4.0_05_OpenAPI.yaml` (the contract; V4.0 file name of `openapi.yaml`), Deliverable 6 workbook (sheets *Endpoint Inventory* and *API-DB Field Map*), Deliverable 4 `run_results.txt` and Deliverable 8 validation evidence. *(V4.0)*

## 1. Scope and baseline

The contract covers every Phase 1 action in the V3.3 Action List. It contains **no** operation for refunds, partial payments, taxes, re-evaluation, moderation queues, WhatsApp, payroll, library, placement, admissions, hostel, transport, alumni, research or external API governance (script check over all paths and operation names: 0 hits). Phase 2 and 3 actions are not used by any operation (0 found).

Three operations exist but are **blocked** until a decision is made, because the Action List gives no action code for them and none is invented here:

| Operation | Blocked by |
|---|---|
| `submitGovernanceItem` | OD-121 <span class="b bad">open</span> — who submits a quality or legal item |
| `placeLegalHold`, `releaseLegalHold` | OD-34 <span class="b bad">open</span> — who places and releases legal holds |

## 2. Conventions

| Topic | Rule | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Base path | `/v1/tenants/{tenantId}/…` for tenant APIs; `/v1/platform/…` for platform roles; `/v1/internal/…` for service-to-service; `/v1/webhooks/…` for the gateway | PTD-61; tenant in path is OD-75 <span class="b bad">open</span> |
| Host | `https://{subdomain}.{baseDomain}`; token tenant must equal subdomain and path tenant, else 404 | TE-07 |
| Versioning | Major version in the path; additive changes only within v1 | PQ-71 |
| Authentication | `bearerAuth`: Keycloak OIDC JWT. `serviceAuth`: client credentials + mTLS for `/v1/internal`. `gatewaySignature`: provider signature on webhooks | OD-10, OD-80 <span class="b bad">open</span> |
| Sign-in | Login, first-login password reset and password change happen in Keycloak, not this API. `POST /v1/internal/identity-events` records them for audit | TD-11 <span class="b warn">proposed</span> |
| Active role | One active role per token. `POST /v1/tenants/{tenantId}/me/active-role` (`switchRole`) takes `{ "assignmentId": "<uuid of an existing app.user_role_assignment owned by the caller>" }` — never a bare role name — and, after validating ownership/tenant/status/effective-dates/scope, returns the updated `CurrentUser` (active role, active assignment id, scope, institution/department, eligible assignments, action codes) and re-checks scope on every subsequent call [PQ-17] | V3.3 |
| JSON | camelCase names mirroring Document 1 snake_case columns; `id` = primary key; `version` = `row_version` | — |
| Money | String with pattern `^-?\d{1,12}\.\d{2}$` (maps to `numeric(14,2)`); INR only | PTD-06 |
| Dates and times | `date` = `YYYY-MM-DD` (tenant time zone); timestamps RFC 3339 UTC | PTD-05 |
| Pagination | Cursor: `limit`, `cursor`; response `pageInfo {limit, nextCursor, hasMore, totalCount?}` | Page sizes OD-76 <span class="b bad">open</span> |
| Concurrency | GET of one resource returns `ETag`; PATCH and state changes need `If-Match`; mismatch → 409 VERSION_CONFLICT | PTD-08 |
| Idempotency | `Idempotency-Key` header: required on 45 operations, accepted on 31 create operations; scopes in §7 | TE-08, PQ-48 |
| Correlation | `X-Correlation-Id` echoed on every response and recorded in audit | PQ-65 |
| Errors | `ErrorResponse` envelope (§6) | Envelope OD-74 <span class="b bad">open</span> |
| Rate limits | 429 RATE_LIMITED with `retryAfterSeconds` | Numbers OD-77 <span class="b bad">open</span> |
| Operation metadata | Every operation carries `x-action-codes`, `x-scope`, `x-sensitive`, `x-audit`, `x-idempotency`, `x-tables`, `x-error-codes`; open items carry `x-open-decision` | — |

## 3. Authorization model

1. **Action codes are the permission.** Each operation lists the V3.3 action codes that allow it (`x-action-codes`). A caller may call it if their **active role** holds one of those actions in the Action List; the roles column in §4 is derived from the "Who can do it" column, not written by hand.
2. **Scope.** `x-scope` repeats the action's "Limited to" rule (for example own department, own children, assigned sections). The service enforces it; with PTD-I1 approved, the database also filters by institution (Document 1 §2.2).
3. **R-1 (Product Owner):** an action includes the reads it needs. List and get operations therefore name the write action as well as any view action.
4. **Management oversight (V3.3):** 99 read operations also accept `TENANT_OVERSIGHT_VIEW` (read-only, whole tenant).
5. **Approvals:** one operation, `POST /approval-requests/{id}/decision` (`decideApproval`), lists all 35 approval actions; the server checks that the caller holds **the action of the current step** and is not the requester (NOT_CURRENT_APPROVER). Missing approvers block the request; nothing is skipped or auto-approved [D-002, C7].
6. **Platform roles** use `/v1/platform`. They never read tenant data through tenant paths, except Support under an active 24-hour grant (`openSupportSession`); which reads count as support-readable is OD-79 <span class="b bad">open</span> (operations marked `x-support-readable`).
7. **Denials** return 403 PERMISSION_DENIED and write a DENIED audit event; records outside the caller's tenant or concealed by scope return 404.
8. **Active role is server-derived, never client-supplied.** The active role, scope and permissions on every request come from the caller's current token/session context, established by the last successful `switchRole` call — never from a role or scope value a client sends in a request body, query string or header. `switchRole` itself only accepts an `assignmentId`; the server looks up that assignment's role and scope server-side rather than trusting any role/scope the client names.

### 3.1 Mode-dependent authorization (carried from §5.63.4)

| Action | Roles holding it | Who is valid when |
|---|---|---|
| `RESULT_PUBLISH` | Controller, College Admin, School Principal, School Admin | Controller: college on an EDU 360 university. College Admin: standalone or external-university college. School roles: school only. |
| `RESULT_WITHDRAW` | Controller, College Admin, School Principal | College Admin needs Principal approval (202) |
| `FEE_POLICY_SET` | Finance Director, Management, Principals | Finance Director: university. Management, else Principal: standalone (OD-126). |
| `FEE_EXCEPTION_APPROVE_FIRST` | Finance Director, Management | Step skipped if no Management role |
| `PERIOD_UNLOCK_APPROVE` | Principals, Finance Director | Finance Director only for the University Accountant's months |
| `STAFF_LEAVE_VERIFY_HR` | HR Manager, University HR Manager | By staff group |
| `ATTENDANCE_CORRECTION_APPROVE` | HOD, School Principal, Vice-Principal (School) | HOD in colleges. School roles in schools. |
| `PRINCIPAL_LEAVE_APPROVE` | Management, Registrar, Vice-Chancellor | The one set per institution (OD-106) |
| `STAFF_RECORD_MANAGE` / `LEAVE_BALANCE_CREDIT` | HR roles, School Admin | School Admin only in schools |
| `LOSS_OF_PAY_CONFIRM` | HR Manager, School Admin | Same split. HR Executive cannot. |


## 4. Endpoint inventory

All 404 operations, grouped by the first path segment. Roles are the union of "Who can do it" for the operation's actions (V3.3); `x-scope` limits them further. Idempotency: R = required, S = supported. Sensitivity follows Document 1 (P personal, F financial, H health/discipline).

## 6. Schemas and errors

### 6.1 Common types (carried from §5.61.1)

| Schema | Definition | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Id` | string, UUID, lowercase | PTD-01 |
| `Timestamp` | string, RFC 3339 UTC with `Z`, milliseconds | Server-set for `createdAt`, `updatedAt` |
| `BusinessDate` | string `YYYY-MM-DD` | Tenant time zone, never converted |
| `Decimal2` | string `^\d{1,12}\.\d{2}$` | Percentages, marks. Marks use `^\d{1,4}\.\d{2}$` (PTD-94). |
| `Money` | `{ amount: Decimal2, currency?: "INR" }` | `currency` may be omitted in v1. Any other value → 422 `CURRENCY_NOT_SUPPORTED`. |
| `ActorRef` | `{ userId: Id, displayName: string, activeRole: string }` |  |
| `ResourceMeta` | `id`, `version` (int ≥ 1), `createdAt`, `updatedAt`, `createdBy: ActorRef` | `version` is the ETag source |
| `PageInfo` | `limit` int, `nextCursor` string or null, `hasMore` bool, `totalCount` int or null |  |
| `Warning` | `{ code, field?, message, context? }` | PTD-90. Non-blocking. |
| `FieldError` | `{ field (JSON pointer), code, message, constraint? }` | Codes in §5.7.1 |
| `ErrorResponse` | `{ error: { code, message, status, correlationId, auditId?, retryable, timestamp, details?, retryAfterSeconds?, context? } }` | PTD-73. OD-74 pending. |
| `Collection<T>` | `{ items: T[], page: PageInfo }` | Every list |
| `JobRef` | `jobId`, `type`, `status`, `createdAt`, `finishedAt?`, `lastErrorCode?`, `result?` | Public states collapse `RETRY_WAIT`→`RUNNING`, `DEAD`→`FAILED` |
| `FileRef` | `fileId`, `originalFilename`, `contentType`, `sizeBytes`, `scanStatus` | **Never a storage key** |
| `StatusChange` | `from`, `to`, `reason?`, `changedAt`, `changedBy: ActorRef` |  |
| `DownloadLink` | `{ url, expiresAt }` | 15 minutes for files [TE-11] |
| `InstitutionRef`, `StudentRef`, `StaffRef`, `SubjectRef` | `id` + display fields | Sensitive fields only with the specific action |


### 6.2 Resource schemas

250 resource schemas are generated from Document 1 tables (each carries `x-table`); 95 further schemas are commands, envelopes and composites. Field-level cross-validation (§8) maps every resource property to a column. ### 6.3 Error envelope

```json
{"error": {"code": "FEE_STRUCTURE_LOCKED", "message": "Only a DRAFT structure can change.", "status": 409,
  "correlationId": "…", "auditId": null, "retryable": false, "timestamp": "2026-10-27T09:30:00Z",
  "retryAfterSeconds": null, "details": [], "context": {}}}
```

`retryable = true` means the same unchanged request may succeed later (carried rule P5-1).

## 7. Idempotency scopes

The key is stored as `(tenant, scope, caller, key)` with a hash of the request; the same key with a different body returns 409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED, a request still running returns 409 REQUEST_IN_PROGRESS. Key retention is OD-39 <span class="b bad">open</span>. The same 55 scopes are loaded into `ref.idempotency_scope`.

## 8. Key business flows

**Fee payment (PO-A1 approved; TD-05 <span class="b warn">proposed</span>).** `startFeePayment` (key required) creates the payment and its intent items and returns the gateway order. The browser's "success" changes nothing. `receivePaymentWebhook` (gateway signature) or a server check confirms; the service then settles installments in due-date order only when fully covered, adds any remainder as credit and notifies the Accountant, and issues one receipt in the same transaction. Response `PaymentConfirmed` returns allocations, `creditAdded`, the receipt and warnings. `markPaymentPaidManually` needs a gateway reference. Duplicate events return the original outcome.

**Approvals.** Submitting operations (`submitMarks`, `submitFeeStructure`, `applyLeave`, `submitPolicy`, …) create an approval request routed by the V3.3 chains (Document 1 §8). `decideApproval` approves, rejects (remark required) or, for a mentor on leave over 2 days, forwards. `bulkApprove` excludes DPO approvals [C12].

**Go-live.** `getGoLiveCheck` lists blockers; `activateTenant` and the `INSTITUTION_ACTIVATE` decision return 409 GO_LIVE_BLOCKED while a Principal's-leave approver (or, for a university, the Vice-Chancellor's) is missing. Approvers are set with `setPrincipalLeaveApprover`, `setVcLeaveApprover`, `setDpcLeaveApprover` (who may set the last is OD-135 <span class="b bad">open</span>).

**Results.** `publishResults` is refused (409 RESULT_NOT_APPROVED) until every submission is Principal-approved, and only by the publisher valid for the institution's mode. `withdrawResults` creates a withdrawal request; for a College Admin it needs Principal approval [Q4].

### 11.3 Assumptions and risks (carried)

| ID | Assumption | If wrong |
|---|---|---|
| AS-A1 | The tenant stays in the path (PTD-61) | Mechanical path change (OD-75) |
| AS-A2 | Errors use the custom envelope | Error schema and client change (OD-74) |
| AS-A3 | Keycloak issues OIDC tokens with a stable subject and role claims | Auth contract changes (OD-80) |
| AS-A4 | The gateway is Razorpay with signed webhooks and order metadata | Payment contract changes (OD-29) |
| AS-A5 | The proposed read gates (PTD-82, PTD-83, PTD-107) are approved | Action codes must be added to the Action List |
| AS-A6 | Cursor pagination suits every list | Some report lists may need offset or export-only |
| AS-A7 | A hand-tallied 219 / 32 / 11 action count is correct | Coverage tables change |
| AS-A8 | No existing API or frontend exists | Reconcile with the real routes before adopting |


| ID | Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AR-1 | A dozen read endpoints rest on proposed gates, so the app can ship with unreviewed access rules | High | High | Approve OD-119 first. Fail the build on any operation with no `x-action-codes` and no `x-open-decision`. |
| AR-2 | Payment flow needs schema changes (D18, D19, D31) that are not approved | Medium | High | Decide before the finance module. Webhook and reconciliation tests. |
| AR-3 | Mode-dependent permissions implemented by action code only | Medium | High | §5.63.4 wrong-mode tests |
| AR-4 | The OpenAPI file and the written tables drift apart | High | Medium | Generate one from the other |
| AR-5 | `warnings[]` and `Money` strings are not honoured by the frontend library | Medium | Medium | Contract tests (§5.65 group 10) |
| AR-6 | Results-day publish time grows beyond one request | Medium | High | Load test. Documented fall-back to 202 plus a job. |
| AR-7 | Receipt-sequence lock contention at fee deadlines | Medium | High | Load test at 10×. Alternative designs ready. |
| AR-8 | Clients rely on enum values that later grow | Low | Medium | PTD-77 rule: ignore unknown enum values |
| AR-9 | Search and reports leak across tenants or institutions | Low | Very high | Mandatory server filter, ST-16, ST-20, T5-06 |
| AR-10 | Hand-built coverage counts are wrong | Medium | Low | Script comparison (§5.65 TC-1 to TC-7) |


### 11.4 Findings that changed this document

| ID | Topic | Severity | Correction |
|---|---|---|---|
| F-01 | Document 2 PDF is mis-assembled | MAJOR | Corrected Document 2 is a separate, self-contained API document that references Document 1 instead of repeating it. |
| F-02 | API Parts 1–4 are missing from Document 2 | CRITICAL | Endpoint inventory rebuilt for every Phase 1 action from the V3.3 Action List (404 operations). Original Part 1–4 wording is not reconstructed; open decisions known only by number are listed as "text not supplied". |
| F-03 | Original OpenAPI deliverable covered only 14 operations | CRITICAL | Complete OpenAPI 3.1.0 file generated (404 operations, 310 paths, 344 schemas — *V4.0: the final file has 345 component schemas; the 344 in this historical finding text pre-dates the last build*) and validated with openapi-spec-validator 0.9.0. |
| F-05 | Part and section numbering is inconsistent | MINOR | Corrected documents use one continuous numbering; corrections are merged into the sections they change. |
| F-06 | Role count: documents follow the 70-role V3.2 grid; V3.3 has 71 roles | MAJOR | Role data, user_type GOVERNANCE, leave-approver settings and the GBA read surface added. |
| F-15 | Institution boundary rests on service checks only (OD-03) | MAJOR | Proposed PTD-I1: restrictive RLS policy on institution_id (fail-closed); tested DB-06, DB-56, DB-57. Applies only if OD-03 is approved. |
| F-23 | Phase 1 actions without an endpoint (incl. sign-in actions) | CRITICAL | coverage.py: 234 of 234 Phase 1 V3.3 actions mapped to at least one operation; 0 unknown codes; 0 Phase 2/3 codes used. |
| F-24 | No program-rules endpoints | MAJOR | list/create/get/update/activate ProgramRuleSet operations. |
| F-25 | Retention-change approval had no request record (RETENTION_CHANGE_APPROVE) | MAJOR | platform.retention_change_request + requestRetentionChange / decideRetentionChange; requester stays OD-130. |

| F-29 | No usage-analytics endpoint (Action 176) | MAJOR | getUsageAnalytics. |
| F-30 | HR leave-balance read had no view action | CORRECT | listLeaveBalances gated by LEAVE_BALANCE_CREDIT and the HR actions under R-1. |
| F-31 | Governance item submission has no action code | CLARIFY | submitGovernanceItem is in the contract but marked x-blocked-by OD-121; no action invented. |
| F-32 | Individual personal-data deletion conflicts with "no hard delete" | CLARIFY | Kept open; data_request supports DELETION type but completion semantics are not defined. |
| F-33 | Who places and releases legal holds | CLARIFY | placeLegalHold / releaseLegalHold present but blocked (x-blocked-by OD-34); no action invented. |
| F-34 | No per-tenant usage endpoint for billing | MAJOR | getTenantUsage (platform). |
| F-35 | Sign-in actions (AUTH_*) had no contract | MAJOR | Login, first-login reset and password change stay in Keycloak; recordIdentityEvent (service auth) records them for audit. Fronting model stays OD-80/OD-10. |
| F-37 | One correction row per entry vs one approval per batch | MAJOR | attendance_correction_request (header, one approval) + attendance_correction (lines). Lines frozen after DRAFT (K-028 trigger). |
| F-38 | Unpaid payments had no stored targets | CRITICAL | payment_intent_item records the installments quoted at start; allocation only at PAID. |
| F-39 | payment_route keyed by an order created after commit | CRITICAL | gateway_order + platform.payment_route keyed by (provider, gateway_order_id) with payment_id; unmatched events kept in platform.payment_unmatched_event. Amount mismatch follows PO-A1 (tested DB-31 to DB-33). |
| F-40 | idempotency_key.scope CHECK had 6 values; the API needs more | MAJOR | ref.idempotency_scope referenced by FK: 55 scopes covering 76 operations. The 21 scopes of Doc 2 §5.64 are kept by name (TENANT_LIFECYCLE extended); 3 REQUIRED scopes and 31 SUPPORTED create scopes added. |
| F-41 | No withdrawal-request record for results | MAJOR | app.result_withdrawal_request (own approval) instead of three columns on result_publication. |
| N-04 | Document 2 relies on platform.payment_route "in Document 1", which Document 1 does not contain | MAJOR | platform.payment_route defined in corrected Doc 1. |
| N-05 | The original OpenAPI excerpt does not parse | CRITICAL | Replaced by the generated, validated file. |
| N-06 | V3.2 and V3.3 rules absent from both documents | MAJOR | Applied: chains (47 processes), go-live gate (DB-07 to DB-11), GBA approver kind (DB-08), delegation guards (DB-19, DB-20), oversight reads. |
| N-08 | Document 2 open decisions OD-74 to OD-129 are not defined in the supplied text | MAJOR | Register lists each with the context line where it appears; no definitions invented. |
| N-12 | No custom-field design although PQ-03 approves controlled custom fields | CLARIFY | Not designed (would be new feature design). Recorded as OD-138. |


## 12. Appendix: Phase 2 and Phase 3

No endpoints are specified for later phases (carried from §5.69).

| Phase | Module | Actions | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2 | Payroll | 8 | Totals-only approvals. Individual salary views logged. Own-salary-change approvals (Q6). |
| 2 | Library | 10 | One copy to one user. Only the Librarian waives fines. |
| 2 | Placement | 7 | Eligibility re-checked at shortlist. Offers visible only to student, parent and placement team. |
| 2 | Admissions | 7 | Application fee goes through the gateway into the ledger. Applicant login by one-time code. |
| 3 | Hostel / Transport | 2 / 3 | Drivers see only name, photo and pickup point. |
| 3 | Alumni / Research / API governance | 2 / 3 / 1 | No alumni login. Tenant keys never visible to platform roles. |
